event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\nimport { ParticleCanvasProps } from '../types';\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  size: number;\n  baseSize: number;\n  color: string;\n  alpha: number;\n  life: number;\n  maxLife: number;\n  isBurst?: boolean;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas = ({ config, colors, onStatsUpdate }: ParticleCanvasProps) => {\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n  const mouseRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; active: boolean; lastMoveTime: number }>({\n    x: 0,\n    y: 0,\n    active: false,\n    lastMoveTime: 0,\n  });\n  const autoOrbitAngleRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const lastFpsCheckRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n  const configRef = useRef(config);\n  const colorsRef = useRef(colors);\n\n  configRef.current = config;\n  colorsRef.current = colors;\n\n  // 星型を描画するヘルパー\n  const drawStar = (ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cx: number, cy: number, spikes: number, outerRadius: number, innerRadius: number) => {\n    let rot = (Math.PI / 2) * 3;\n    let x = cx;\n    let y = cy;\n    const step = Math.PI / spikes;\n\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - outerRadius);\n    for (let i = 0; i < spikes; i++) {\n      x = cx + Math.cos(rot) * outerRadius;\n      y = cy + Math.sin(rot) * outerRadius;\n      ctx.lineTo(x, y);\n      rot += step;\n\n      x = cx + Math.cos(rot) * innerRadius;\n      y = cy + Math.sin(rot) * innerRadius;\n      ctx.lineTo(x, y);\n      rot += step;\n    }\n    ctx.lineTo(cx, cy - outerRadius);\n    ctx.closePath();\n    ctx.fill();\n  };\n\n  // スパークル（4点十字輝き）を描画するヘルパー\n  const drawSparkle = (ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cx: number, cy: number, size: number) => {\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - size);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx + size, cy);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx, cy + size);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx - size, cy);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx, cy - size);\n    ctx.closePath();\n    ctx.fill();\n  };\n\n  // バースト発生（クリックまたは外部呼び出し）\n  const createBurstAt = (x: number, y: number, count = 45) => {\n    const currentColors = colorsRef.current;\n    const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n      const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / count + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n      const speed = (Math.random() * 6 + 3) * currentConfig.baseSpeed;\n      const randomColor = currentColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * currentColors.length)] || '#00f2fe';\n\n      particlesRef.current.push({\n        x,\n        y,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        size: Math.random() * currentConfig.particleSize * 1.8 + 2,\n        baseSize: currentConfig.particleSize,\n        color: randomColor,\n        alpha: 1,\n        life: 0,\n        maxLife: Math.random() * 50 + 40,\n        isBurst: true,\n      });\n    }\n  };\n\n  // 外部からのバースト発火イベント受け取り\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleTriggerBurst = () => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const x = mouseRef.current.active ? mouseRef.current.x : canvas.width / 2;\n      const y = mouseRef.current.active ? mouseRef.current.y : canvas.height / 2;\n      createBurstAt(x, y, 70);\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleTriggerBurst);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleTriggerBurst);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    if (!canvas) return;\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n    let width = (canvas.width = window.innerWidth);\n    let height = (canvas.height = window.innerHeight);\n\n    // 初期パーティクルの生成\n    const createBaseParticle = (): Particle => {\n      const currentColors = colorsRef.current;\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      const color = currentColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * currentColors.length)] || '#00f2fe';\n      return {\n        x: Math.random() * width,\n        y: Math.random() * height,\n        vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5 * currentConfig.baseSpeed,\n        vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5 * currentConfig.baseSpeed,\n        size: Math.random() * currentConfig.particleSize + 1.5,\n        baseSize: currentConfig.particleSize,\n        color,\n        alpha: Math.random() * 0.6 + 0.4,\n        life: 0,\n        maxLife: Math.random() * 300 + 200,\n        isBurst: false,\n      };\n    };\n\n    particlesRef.current = Array.from({ length: config.particleCount }, createBaseParticle);\n\n    // リサイズハンドラ\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      width = canvas.width = window.innerWidth;\n      height = canvas.height = window.innerHeight;\n    };\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // ポインターイベント\n    const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      mouseRef.current.x = e.clientX;\n      mouseRef.current.y = e.clientY;\n      mouseRef.current.active = true;\n      mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime = performance.now();\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      mouseRef.current.x = e.clientX;\n      mouseRef.current.y = e.clientY;\n      mouseRef.current.active = true;\n      mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime = performance.now();\n      createBurstAt(e.clientX, e.clientY, 40);\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n      mouseRef.current.active = false;\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', handlePointerLeave);\n\n    // メインアニメーションループ\n    const render = () => {\n      const now = performance.now();\n      frameCountRef.current++;\n\n      // 1秒ごとにFPSとアクティブ数を更新\n      if (now - lastFpsCheckRef.current >= 1000) {\n        const fps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (now - lastFpsCheckRef.current));\n        onStatsUpdate?.({\n          fps,\n          activeParticles: particlesRef.current.length,\n        });\n        frameCountRef.current = 0;\n        lastFpsCheckRef.current = now;\n      }\n\n      const curConfig = configRef.current;\n      const curColors = colorsRef.current;\n\n      // トレイル（残像）表現\n      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';\n      const trailAlpha = Math.max(0.04, 1.05 - curConfig.trailLength);\n      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(5, 6, 11, ${trailAlpha})`;\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 加算合成（光彩エフェクト）\n      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n\n      // ターゲット座標の決定（ユーザー操作 or 自動周回デモ）\n      let targetX = mouseRef.current.x;\n      let targetY = mouseRef.current.y;\n      const isIdle = !mouseRef.current.active || now - mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime > 3000;\n\n      if (curConfig.autoOrbit && isIdle) {\n        autoOrbitAngleRef.current += 0.02 * curConfig.baseSpeed;\n        const angle = autoOrbitAngleRef.current;\n        // 滑らかなレムニスケート（8の字）曲線軌道\n        const scale = Math.min(width, height) * 0.32;\n        targetX = width / 2 + (scale * Math.cos(angle)) / (1 + Math.sin(angle) * Math.sin(angle));\n        targetY = height / 2 + (scale * Math.sin(angle) * Math.cos(angle)) / (1 + Math.sin(angle) * Math.sin(angle));\n      }\n\n      // パーティクル数の維持（目標数への調整）\n      const targetCount = curConfig.particleCount;\n      const currentList = particlesRef.current;\n\n      if (currentList.length < targetCount) {\n        const diff = Math.min(10, targetCount - currentList.length);\n        for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {\n          currentList.push(createBaseParticle());\n        }\n      } else if (currentList.length > targetCount + 50) {\n        currentList.splice(0, currentList.length - targetCount);\n      }\n\n      // 各パーティクルの更新と描画\n      for (let i = currentList.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n        const p = currentList[i];\n\n        if (p.isBurst) {\n          p.life++;\n          p.vx *= 0.94;\n          p.vy *= 0.94;\n          p.vy += curConfig.gravity * 0.4;\n          p.x += p.vx;\n          p.y += p.vy;\n          p.alpha = Math.max(0, 1 - p.life / p.maxLife);\n\n          if (p.life >= p.maxLife || p.alpha <= 0.01) {\n            currentList.splice(i, 1);\n            continue;\n          }\n        } else {\n          // 通常パーティクル：マウス/ターゲットへの引力スプリング\n          const dx = targetX - p.x;\n          const dy = targetY - p.y;\n          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n          if (dist > 1) {\n            const force = (curConfig.mouseFollowStrength * 0.08 * curConfig.baseSpeed) / Math.max(dist * 0.04, 1);\n            p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n            p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n          }\n\n          // 速度減衰と重力\n          p.vx *= 0.96;\n          p.vy *= 0.96;\n          p.vy += curConfig.gravity * 0.2;\n\n          p.x += p.vx;\n          p.y += p.vy;\n\n          // 画面外ワープ / バウンド\n          if (p.x < 0) p.x = width;\n          if (p.x > width) p.x = 0;\n          if (p.y < 0) p.y = height;\n          if (p.y > height) p.y = 0;\n        }\n\n        // 描画\n        ctx.save();\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n        ctx.shadowBlur = p.isBurst ? 14 : 8;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n\n        const currentSize = p.isBurst ? p.size : (curConfig.particleSize * (p.size / p.baseSize));\n\n        if (curConfig.shape === 'star') {\n          drawStar(ctx, p.x, p.y, 5, currentSize * 1.5, currentSize * 0.6);\n        } else if (curConfig.shape === 'sparkle') {\n          drawSparkle(ctx, p.x, p.y, currentSize * 1.6);\n        } else {\n          ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, currentSize), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n          ctx.fill();\n        }\n        ctx.restore();\n      }\n\n      // 近接パーティクル同士の光線コネクション描画\n      if (curConfig.connectionLines) {\n        const maxDist = 85;\n        const maxParticlesToCheck = Math.min(currentList.length, 250);\n        ctx.lineWidth = 0.7;\n\n        for (let i = 0; i < maxParticlesToCheck; i++) {\n          const pA = currentList[i];\n          for (let j = i + 1; j < maxParticlesToCheck; j++) {\n            const pB = currentList[j];\n            const dx = pA.x - pB.x;\n            const dy = pA.y - pB.y;\n            const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n            if (dist < maxDist) {\n              const lineAlpha = (1 - dist / maxDist) * 0.35 * Math.min(pA.alpha, pB.alpha);\n              ctx.strokeStyle = pA.color;\n              ctx.globalAlpha = lineAlpha;\n              ctx.beginPath();\n              ctx.moveTo(pA.x, pA.y);\n              ctx.lineTo(pB.x, pB.y);\n              ctx.stroke();\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', handlePointerLeave);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"canvas-container\">\n      <canvas ref={canvasRef} />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ParticleCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef, useImperativeHandle, forwardRef } from 'react';\nimport { ParticleCanvasProps } from '../types';\n\nexport interface ParticleCanvasHandle {\n  triggerBurst: (x?: number, y?: number) => void;\n}\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  size: number;\n  baseSize: number;\n  color: string;\n  alpha: number;\n  life: number;\n  maxLife: number;\n  isBurst?: boolean;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas = forwardRef<ParticleCanvasHandle, ParticleCanvasProps>(\n  ({ config, colors, onStatsUpdate }, ref) => {\n    const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n    const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n    const useRef<number>(0);\n    const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n    const lastFpsCheckRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n    const configRef = useRef(config);\n    const colorsRef = useRef(colors);\n\n    configRef.current = config;\n    colorsRef.current = colors;\n\n    // 星型を描画するヘルパー\n    const drawStar = (\n      ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,\n      cx: number,\n      cy: number,\n      spikes: number,\n      outerRadius: number,\n      innerRadius: number\n    ) => {\n      let rot = (Math.PI / 2) * 3;\n      let x = cx;\n      let y = cy;\n      const step = Math.PI / spikes;\n\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - outerRadius);\n      for (let i = 0; i < spikes; i++) {\n        x = cx + Math.cos(rot) * outerRadius;\n        y = cy + Math.sin(rot) * outerRadius;\n        ctx.lineTo(x, y);\n        rot += step;\n\n        x = cx + Math.cos(rot) * innerRadius;\n        y = cy + Math.sin(rot) * innerRadius;\n        ctx.lineTo(x, y);\n        rot += step;\n      }\n      ctx.lineTo(cx, cy - outerRadius);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.fill();\n    };\n\n    // スパークル（4点十字輝き）を描画するヘルパー\n    const drawSparkle = (ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cx: number, cy: number, size: number) => {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - size);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx + size, cy);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx, cy + size);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx - size, cy);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx, cy - size);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.fill();\n    };\n\n    // バースト発生（クリックまたは外部呼び出し）\n    const createBurstAt = (x: number, y: number, count = 45) => {\n      const currentColors = colorsRef.current;\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / count + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const speed = (Math.random() * 6 + 3) * currentConfig.baseSpeed;\n        const randomColor =\n          currentColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * currentColors.length)] || '#00f2fe';\n\n        particlesRef.current.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n          size: Math.random() * currentConfig.particleSize * 1.8 + 2,\n          baseSize: currentConfig.particleSize,\n          color: randomColor,\n          alpha: 1,\n          life: 0,\n          maxLife: Math.random() * 50 + 40,\n          isBurst: true,\n        });\n      }\n    };\n\n    // 親コンポーネント用命令的ハンドル (useImperativeHandle)\n    useImperativeHandle(\n      ref,\n      () => ({\n        triggerBurst: (customX?: number, customY?: number) => {\n          const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n          if (!canvas) return;\n          const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n          const targetX =\n            customX !== undefined\n              ? customX\n              : mouseRef.current.active\n              ? mouseRef.current.x\n              : rect.width / 2;\n          const targetY =\n            customY !== undefined\n              ? customY\n              : mouseRef.current.active\n              ? mouseRef.current.y\n              : rect.height / 2;\n          createBurstAt(targetX, targetY, 70);\n        },\n      }),\n      []\n    );\n\n    // 外部からのバースト発火イベント受け取り (フォールバック)\n    useEffect(() => {\n      const handleTriggerBurst = () => {\n        const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n        if (!canvas) return;\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const x = mouseRef.current.active ? mouseRef.current.x : rect.width / 2;\n        const y = mouseRef.current.active ? mouseRef.current.y : rect.height / 2;\n        createBurstAt(x, y, 70);\n      };\n\n      window.addEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleTriggerBurst);\n      return () => {\n        window.removeEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleTriggerBurst);\n      };\n    }, []);\n\n    useEffect(() => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) return;\n\n      let animationFrameId: number;\n      let dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      let width = window.innerWidth;\n      let height = window.innerHeight;\n\n      // devicePixelRatio対応のサイズ初期化\n      const updateCanvasSize = () => {\n        dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n        width = window.innerWidth;\n        height = window.innerHeight;\n        canvas.width = Math.floor(width * dpr);\n        canvas.height = Math.floor(height * dpr);\n        canvas.style.width = `${width}px`;\n        canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;\n        ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);\n      };\n\n      updateCanvasSize();\n\n      // 初期パーティクルの生成\n      const createBaseParticle = (): Particle => {\n        const currentColors = colorsRef.current;\n        const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n        const color = currentColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * currentColors.length)] || '#00f2fe';\n        return {\n          x: Math.random() * width,\n          y: Math.random() * height,\n          vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5 * currentConfig.baseSpeed,\n          vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5 * currentConfig.baseSpeed,\n          size: Math.random() * currentConfig.particleSize + 1.5,\n          baseSize: currentConfig.particleSize,\n          color,\n          alpha: Math.random() * 0.6 + 0.4,\n          life: 0,\n          maxLife: Math.random() * 300 + 200,\n          isBurst: false,\n        };\n      };\n\n      particlesRef.current = Array.from({ length: config.particleCount }, createBaseParticle);\n\n      // リサイズハンドラ\n      const handleResize = () => {\n        updateCanvasSize();\n      };\n      window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n      // ポインターイベント：canvas要素に限定しUI操作との干渉を防ぐ\n      const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        mouseRef.current.x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n        mouseRef.current.y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n        mouseRef.current.active = true;\n        mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime = performance.now();\n      };\n\n      const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const px = e.clientX - rect.left;\n        const py = e.clientY - rect.top;\n        mouseRef.current.x = px;\n        mouseRef.current.y = py;\n        mouseRef.current.active = true;\n        mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime = performance.now();\n        createBurstAt(px, py, 45);\n      };\n\n      const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n        mouseRef.current.active = false;\n      };\n\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointerleave', handlePointerLeave);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', handlePointerLeave);\n\n      // メインアニメーションループ\n      const render = () => {\n        const now = performance.now();\n        frameCountRef.current++;\n\n        // 1秒ごとにFPSとアクティブ数を更新\n        if (now - lastFpsCheckRef.current >= 1000) {\n          const fps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (now - lastFpsCheckRef.current));\n          onStatsUpdate?.({\n            fps,\n            activeParticles: particlesRef.current.length,\n          });\n          frameCountRef.current = 0;\n          lastFpsCheckRef.current = now;\n        }\n\n        const curConfig = configRef.current;\n\n        // トレイル（残像）表現\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';\n        const trailAlpha = Math.max(0.04, 1.05 - curConfig.trailLength);\n        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(5, 6, 11, ${trailAlpha})`;\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n        // 加算合成（光彩エフェクト）\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n\n        // ターゲット座標の決定（ユーザー操作 or 自動周回デモ）\n        let targetX = mouseRef.current.x;\n        let targetY = mouseRef.current.y;\n        const isIdle = !mouseRef.current.active || now - mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime > 3000;\n\n        if (curConfig.autoOrbit && isIdle) {\n          autoOrbitAngleRef.current += 0.02 * curConfig.baseSpeed;\n          const angle = autoOrbitAngleRef.current;\n          // 滑らかなレムニスケート（8の字）曲線軌道\n          const scale = Math.min(width, height) * 0.32;\n          targetX = width / 2 + (scale * Math.cos(angle)) / (1 + Math.sin(angle) * Math.sin(angle));\n          targetY = height / 2 + (scale * Math.sin(angle) * Math.cos(angle)) / (1 + Math.sin(angle) * Math.sin(angle));\n        }\n\n        // パーティクル数の維持（目標数への調整）\n        const targetCount = curConfig.particleCount;\n        const currentList = particlesRef.current;\n\n        if (currentList.length < targetCount) {\n          const diff = Math.min(10, targetCount - currentList.length);\n          for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {\n            currentList.push(createBaseParticle());\n          }\n        } else if (currentList.length > targetCount + 50) {\n          currentList.splice(0, currentList.length - targetCount);\n        }\n\n        // 各パーティクルの更新と描画\n        for (let i = currentList.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n          const p = currentList[i];\n\n          if (p.isBurst) {\n            p.life++;\n            p.vx *= 0.94;\n            p.vy *= 0.94;\n            p.vy += curConfig.gravity * 0.4;\n            p.x += p.vx;\n            p.y += p.vy;\n            p.alpha = Math.max(0, 1 - p.life / p.maxLife);\n\n            if (p.life >= p.maxLife || p.alpha <= 0.01) {\n              currentList.splice(i, 1);\n              continue;\n            }\n          } else {\n            // 通常パーティクル：マウス/ターゲットへの引力スプリング\n            const dx = targetX - p.x;\n            const dy = targetY - p.y;\n            const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n            if (dist > 1) {\n              const force =\n                (curConfig.mouseFollowStrength * 0.08 * curConfig.baseSpeed) /\n                Math.max(dist * 0.04, 1);\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n            }\n\n            // 速度減衰と重力\n            p.vx *= 0.96;\n            p.vy *= 0.96;\n            p.vy += curConfig.gravity * 0.2;\n\n            p.x += p.vx;\n            p.y += p.vy;\n\n            // 画面外ワープ / バウンド\n            if (p.x < 0) p.x = width;\n            if (p.x > width) p.x = 0;\n            if (p.y < 0) p.y = height;\n            if (p.y > height) p.y = 0;\n          }\n\n          // 描画\n          ctx.save();\n          ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n          ctx.shadowBlur = p.isBurst ? 14 : 8;\n          ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n\n          const currentSize = p.isBurst\n            ? p.size\n            : curConfig.particleSize * (p.size / p.baseSize);\n\n          if (curConfig.shape === 'star') {\n            drawStar(ctx, p.x, p.y, 5, currentSize * 1.5, currentSize * 0.6);\n          } else if (curConfig.shape === 'sparkle') {\n            drawSparkle(ctx, p.x, p.y, currentSize * 1.6);\n          } else {\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, currentSize), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n            ctx.fill();\n          }\n          ctx.restore();\n        }\n\n        // 近接パーティクル同士の光線コネクション描画\n        if (curConfig.connectionLines) {\n          const maxDist = 85;\n          const maxParticlesToCheck = Math.min(currentList.length, 250);\n          ctx.lineWidth = 0.7;\n\n          for (let i = 0; i < maxParticlesToCheck; i++) {\n            const pA = currentList[i];\n            for (let j = i + 1; j < maxParticlesToCheck; j++) {\n              const pB = currentList[j];\n              const dx = pA.x - pB.x;\n              const dy = pA.y - pB.y;\n              const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n              if (dist < maxDist) {\n                const lineAlpha = (1 - dist / maxDist) * 0.35 * Math.min(pA.alpha, pB.alpha);\n                ctx.strokeStyle = pA.color;\n                ctx.globalAlpha = lineAlpha;\n                ctx.beginPath();\n                ctx.moveTo(pA.x, pA.y);\n                ctx.lineTo(pB.x, pB.y);\n                ctx.stroke();\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n      };\n\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n      return () => {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n        window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n        canvas.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n        canvas.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n        canvas.removeEventListener('pointerleave', handlePointerLeave);\n        canvas.removeEventListener('pointercancel', handlePointerLeave);\n      };\n    }, []);\n\n    return (\n      <div className=\"canvas-container\">\n        <canvas ref={canvasRef} />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n);\n\nParticleCanvas.displayName = 'ParticleCanvas';\n\nexport default ParticleCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef, useImperativeHandle, forwardRef } from 'react';\nimport { ParticleCanvasProps, CanvasHandle } from '../types';\n\nexport type ParticleCanvasHandle = CanvasHandle;\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  size: number;\n  baseSize: number;\n  color: string;\n  alpha: number;\n  life: number;\n  maxLife: number;\n  isBurst?: boolean;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas = forwardRef<ParticleCanvasHandle, ParticleCanvasProps>(\n  ({ config, colors, onStatsUpdate }, ref) => {\n    const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n    const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n    const mouseRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; active: boolean; lastMoveTime: number }>({\n      x: 0,\n      y: 0,\n      active: false,\n      lastMoveTime: 0,\n    });\n    const autoOrbitAngleRef = useRef<number>(0);\n    const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n    const lastFpsCheckRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n    const configRef = useRef(config);\n    const colorsRef = useRef(colors);\n\n    configRef.current = config;\n    colorsRef.current = colors;\n\n    // 星型を描画するヘルパー\n    const drawStar = (\n      ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,\n      cx: number,\n      cy: number,\n      spikes: number,\n      outerRadius: number,\n      innerRadius: number\n    ) => {\n      let rot = (Math.PI / 2) * 3;\n      let x = cx;\n      let y = cy;\n      const step = Math.PI / spikes;\n\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - outerRadius);\n      for (let i = 0; i < spikes; i++) {\n        x = cx + Math.cos(rot) * outerRadius;\n        y = cy + Math.sin(rot) * outerRadius;\n        ctx.lineTo(x, y);\n        rot += step;\n\n        x = cx + Math.cos(rot) * innerRadius;\n        y = cy + Math.sin(rot) * innerRadius;\n        ctx.lineTo(x, y);\n        rot += step;\n      }\n      ctx.lineTo(cx, cy - outerRadius);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.fill();\n    };\n\n    // スパークル（4点十字輝き）を描画するヘルパー\n    const drawSparkle = (ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cx: number, cy: number, size: number) => {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - size);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx + size, cy);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx, cy + size);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx - size, cy);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx, cy - size);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.fill();\n    };\n\n    // バースト発生（クリックまたは外部呼び出し）\n    const createBurstAt = (x: number, y: number, count = 45) => {\n      const currentColors = colorsRef.current;\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / count + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const speed = (Math.random() * 6 + 3) * currentConfig.baseSpeed;\n        const randomColor =\n          currentColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * currentColors.length)] || '#00f2fe';\n\n        particlesRef.current.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n          size: Math.random() * currentConfig.particleSize * 1.8 + 2,\n          baseSize: currentConfig.particleSize,\n          color: randomColor,\n          alpha: 1,\n          life: 0,\n          maxLife: Math.random() * 50 + 40,\n          isBurst: true,\n        });\n      }\n    };\n\n    // 親コンポーネント用命令的ハンドル (useImperativeHandle)\n    useImperativeHandle(\n      ref,\n      () => ({\n        triggerBurst: (customX?: number, customY?: number, count = 70) => {\n          const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n          if (!canvas) return;\n          const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n          const targetX =\n            customX !== undefined\n              ? customX\n              : mouseRef.current.active\n              ? mouseRef.current.x\n              : rect.width / 2;\n          const targetY =\n            customY !== undefined\n              ? customY\n              : mouseRef.current.active\n              ? mouseRef.current.y\n              : rect.height / 2;\n          createBurstAt(targetX, targetY, count);\n        },\n        resetParticles: () => {\n          particlesRef.current = [];\n        },\n      }),\n      []\n    );\n\n    // 外部からのバースト発火イベント受け取り (フォールバック)\n    useEffect(() => {\n      const handleTriggerBurst = () => {\n        const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n        if (!canvas) return;\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const x = mouseRef.current.active ? mouseRef.current.x : rect.width / 2;\n        const y = mouseRef.current.active ? mouseRef.current.y : rect.height / 2;\n        createBurstAt(x, y, 70);\n      };\n\n      window.addEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleTriggerBurst);\n      return () => {\n        window.removeEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleTriggerBurst);\n      };\n    }, []);\n\n    useEffect(() => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) return;\n\n      let animationFrameId: number;\n      let dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      let width = window.innerWidth;\n      let height = window.innerHeight;\n\n      // devicePixelRatio対応のサイズ初期化\n      const updateCanvasSize = () => {\n        dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n        width = window.innerWidth;\n        height = window.innerHeight;\n        canvas.width = Math.floor(width * dpr);\n        canvas.height = Math.floor(height * dpr);\n        canvas.style.width = `${width}px`;\n        canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;\n        ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);\n      };\n\n      updateCanvasSize();\n\n      // 初期パーティクルの生成\n      const createBaseParticle = (): Particle => {\n        const currentColors = colorsRef.current;\n        const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n        const color = currentColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * currentColors.length)] || '#00f2fe';\n        return {\n          x: Math.random() * width,\n          y: Math.random() * height,\n          vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5 * currentConfig.baseSpeed,\n          vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5 * currentConfig.baseSpeed,\n          size: Math.random() * currentConfig.particleSize + 1.5,\n          baseSize: currentConfig.particleSize,\n          color,\n          alpha: Math.random() * 0.6 + 0.4,\n          life: 0,\n          maxLife: Math.random() * 300 + 200,\n          isBurst: false,\n        };\n      };\n\n      particlesRef.current = Array.from({ length: config.particleCount }, createBaseParticle);\n\n      // リサイズハンドラ\n      const handleResize = () => {\n        updateCanvasSize();\n      };\n      window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n      // ポインターイベント：canvas要素に限定しUI操作との干渉を防ぐ\n      const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        mouseRef.current.x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n        mouseRef.current.y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n        mouseRef.current.active = true;\n        mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime = performance.now();\n      };\n\n      const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const px = e.clientX - rect.left;\n        const py = e.clientY - rect.top;\n        mouseRef.current.x = px;\n        mouseRef.current.y = py;\n        mouseRef.current.active = true;\n        mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime = performance.now();\n        createBurstAt(px, py, 45);\n      };\n\n      const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n        mouseRef.current.active = false;\n      };\n\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointerleave', handlePointerLeave);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', handlePointerLeave);\n\n      // メインアニメーションループ\n      const render = () => {\n        const now = performance.now();\n        frameCountRef.current++;\n\n        // 1秒ごとにFPSとアクティブ数を更新\n        if (now - lastFpsCheckRef.current >= 1000) {\n          const fps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (now - lastFpsCheckRef.current));\n          onStatsUpdate?.({\n            fps,\n            activeParticles: particlesRef.current.length,\n          });\n          frameCountRef.current = 0;\n          lastFpsCheckRef.current = now;\n        }\n\n        const curConfig = configRef.current;\n\n        // トレイル（残像）表現\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';\n        const trailAlpha = Math.max(0.04, 1.05 - curConfig.trailLength);\n        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(5, 6, 11, ${trailAlpha})`;\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n        // 加算合成（光彩エフェクト）\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n\n        // ターゲット座標の決定（ユーザー操作 or 自動周回デモ）\n        let targetX = mouseRef.current.x;\n        let targetY = mouseRef.current.y;\n        const isIdle = !mouseRef.current.active || now - mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime > 3000;\n\n        if (curConfig.autoOrbit && isIdle) {\n          autoOrbitAngleRef.current += 0.02 * curConfig.baseSpeed;\n          const angle = autoOrbitAngleRef.current;\n          // 滑らかなレムニスケート（8の字）曲線軌道\n          const scale = Math.min(width, height) * 0.32;\n          targetX = width / 2 + (scale * Math.cos(angle)) / (1 + Math.sin(angle) * Math.sin(angle));\n          targetY = height / 2 + (scale * Math.sin(angle) * Math.cos(angle)) / (1 + Math.sin(angle) * Math.sin(angle));\n        }\n\n        // パーティクル数の維持（目標数への調整）\n        const targetCount = curConfig.particleCount;\n        const currentList = particlesRef.current;\n\n        if (currentList.length < targetCount) {\n          const diff = Math.min(10, targetCount - currentList.length);\n          for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {\n            currentList.push(createBaseParticle());\n          }\n        } else if (currentList.length > targetCount + 50) {\n          currentList.splice(0, currentList.length - targetCount);\n        }\n\n        // 各パーティクルの更新と描画\n        for (let i = currentList.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n          const p = currentList[i];\n\n          if (p.isBurst) {\n            p.life++;\n            p.vx *= 0.94;\n            p.vy *= 0.94;\n            p.vy += curConfig.gravity * 0.4;\n            p.x += p.vx;\n            p.y += p.vy;\n            p.alpha = Math.max(0, 1 - p.life / p.maxLife);\n\n            if (p.life >= p.maxLife || p.alpha <= 0.01) {\n              currentList.splice(i, 1);\n              continue;\n            }\n          } else {\n            // 通常パーティクル：マウス/ターゲットへの引力スプリング\n            const dx = targetX - p.x;\n            const dy = targetY - p.y;\n            const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n            if (dist > 1) {\n              const force =\n                (curConfig.mouseFollowStrength * 0.08 * curConfig.baseSpeed) /\n                Math.max(dist * 0.04, 1);\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n            }\n\n            // 速度減衰と重力\n            p.vx *= 0.96;\n            p.vy *= 0.96;\n            p.vy += curConfig.gravity * 0.2;\n\n            p.x += p.vx;\n            p.y += p.vy;\n\n    const currentSize = p.isBurst\n            ? p.size\n            : curConfig.particleSize * (p.size / p.baseSize);\n\n          if (curConfig.shape === 'star') {\n            drawStar(ctx, p.x, p.y, 5, currentSize * 1.5, currentSize * 0.6);\n          } else if (curConfig.shape === 'sparkle') {\n            drawSparkle(ctx, p.x, p.y, currentSize * 1.6);\n          } else {\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, currentSize), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n            ctx.fill();\n          }\n          ctx.restore();\n        }\n\n        // 近接パーティクル同士の光線コネクション描画\n        if (curConfig.connectionLines) {\n          const maxDist = 85;\n          const maxParticlesToCheck = Math.min(currentList.length, 250);\n          ctx.lineWidth = 0.7;\n\n          for (let i = 0; i < maxParticlesToCheck; i++) {\n            const pA = currentList[i];\n            for (let j = i + 1; j < maxParticlesToCheck; j++) {\n              const pB = currentList[j];\n              const dx = pA.x - pB.x;\n              const dy = pA.y - pB.y;\n              const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n              if (dist < maxDist) {\n                const lineAlpha = (1 - dist / maxDist) * 0.35 * Math.min(pA.alpha, pB.alpha);\n                ctx.strokeStyle = pA.color;\n                ctx.globalAlpha = lineAlpha;\n                ctx.beginPath();\n                ctx.moveTo(pA.x, pA.y);\n                ctx.lineTo(pB.x, pB.y);\n                ctx.stroke();\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n      };\n\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n      return () => {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n        window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n        canvas.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n        canvas.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n number;\n  connectionLines: boolean;\n  mouseFollowStrength: number;\n  shape: 'circle' | 'star' | 'sparkle';\n  gravity: number;\n  autoOrbit: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface PresetItem {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  colors: string[];\n  description: string;\n  config: Partial<ParticleConfig>;\n}\n\nexport interface StatsData {\n  fps: number;\n  activeParticles: number;\n}\n\nexport interface ParticleCanvasHandle {\n  triggerBurst: (x?: number, y?: number) => void;\n  resetParticles?: () => void;\n}\n\nexport type CanvasHandle = ParticleCanvasHandle;\n\nexport type ParticleCanvasRef = ParticleCanvasHandle;\n\nexport interface ParticleCanvasProps {\n  config: ParticleConfig;\n  colors: string[];\n  onStatsUpdate?: (stats: StatsData) => void;\n}\n\nexport interface ControlPanelProps {\n  config: ParticleConfig;\n  presets?: PresetItem[];\n  currentPresetId: string;\n  onSelectPreset?: (preset: PresetItem) => void;\n  onChangeConfig?: <K extends keyof ParticleConfig>(key: K, value: ParticleConfig[K]) => void;\n  onTriggerBurst?: () => void;\n  isExpanded: boolean;\n  onToggleExpand?: () => void;\n}","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/components/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef, useImperativeHandle, forwardRef } from 'react';\nimport { ParticleCanvasProps, CanvasHandle } from '../types';\n\nexport type ParticleCanvasHandle = CanvasHandle;\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  size: number;\n  baseSize: number;\n  color: string;\n  alpha: number;\n  life: number;\n  maxLife: number;\n  isBurst?: boolean;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas = forwardRef<ParticleCanvasHandle, ParticleCanvasProps>(\n  ({ config, colors, onStatsUpdate }, ref) => {\n    const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n    const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n    const mouseRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; active: boolean; lastMoveTime: number }>({\n      x: 0,\n      y: 0,\n      active: false,\n      lastMoveTime: 0,\n    });\n    const autoOrbitAngleRef = useRef<number>(0);\n    const frameCountRef = useRef<number>(0);\n    const lastFpsCheckRef = useRef<number>(performance.now());\n    const configRef = useRef(config);\n    const colorsRef = useRef(colors);\n\n    configRef.current = config;\n    colorsRef.current = colors;\n\n    // 星型を描画するヘルパー\n    const drawStar = (\n      ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,\n      cx: number,\n      cy: number,\n      spikes: number,\n      outerRadius: number,\n      innerRadius: number\n    ) => {\n      let rot = (Math.PI / 2) * 3;\n      let x = cx;\n      let y = cy;\n      const step = Math.PI / spikes;\n\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - outerRadius);\n      for (let i = 0; i < spikes; i++) {\n        x = cx + Math.cos(rot) * outerRadius;\n        y = cy + Math.sin(rot) * outerRadius;\n        ctx.lineTo(x, y);\n        rot += step;\n\n        x = cx + Math.cos(rot) * innerRadius;\n        y = cy + Math.sin(rot) * innerRadius;\n        ctx.lineTo(x, y);\n        rot += step;\n      }\n      ctx.lineTo(cx, cy - outerRadius);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.fill();\n    };\n\n    // スパークル（4点十字輝き）を描画するヘルパー\n    const drawSparkle = (ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cx: number, cy: number, size: number) => {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(cx, cy - size);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx + size, cy);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx, cy + size);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx - size, cy);\n      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(cx, cy, cx, cy - size);\n      ctx.closePath();\n      ctx.fill();\n    };\n\n    // バースト発生（クリックまたは外部呼び出し）\n    const createBurstAt = (x: number, y: number, count = 45) => {\n      const currentColors = colorsRef.current;\n      const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / count + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;\n        const speed = (Math.random() * 6 + 3) * currentConfig.baseSpeed;\n        const randomColor =\n          currentColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * currentColors.length)] || '#00f2fe';\n\n        particlesRef.current.push({\n          x,\n          y,\n          vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n          vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n          size: Math.random() * currentConfig.particleSize * 1.8 + 2,\n          baseSize: currentConfig.particleSize,\n          color: randomColor,\n          alpha: 1,\n          life: 0,\n          maxLife: Math.random() * 50 + 40,\n          isBurst: true,\n        });\n      }\n    };\n\n    // 親コンポーネント用命令的ハンドル (useImperativeHandle)\n    useImperativeHandle(\n      ref,\n      () => ({\n        triggerBurst: (customX?: number, customY?: number, count = 70) => {\n          const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n          if (!canvas) return;\n          const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n          const targetX =\n            customX !== undefined\n              ? customX\n              : mouseRef.current.active\n              ? mouseRef.current.x\n              : rect.width / 2;\n          const targetY =\n            customY !== undefined\n              ? customY\n              : mouseRef.current.active\n              ? mouseRef.current.y\n              : rect.height / 2;\n          createBurstAt(targetX, targetY, count);\n        },\n        resetParticles: () => {\n          particlesRef.current = [];\n        },\n      }),\n      []\n    );\n\n    // 外部からのバースト発火イベント受け取り (フォールバック)\n    useEffect(() => {\n      const handleTriggerBurst = () => {\n        const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n        if (!canvas) return;\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const x = mouseRef.current.active ? mouseRef.current.x : rect.width / 2;\n        const y = mouseRef.current.active ? mouseRef.current.y : rect.height / 2;\n        createBurstAt(x, y, 70);\n      };\n\n      window.addEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleTriggerBurst);\n      return () => {\n        window.removeEventListener('trigger-particle-burst', handleTriggerBurst);\n      };\n    }, []);\n\n    useEffect(() => {\n      const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n      if (!canvas) return;\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!ctx) return;\n\n      let animationFrameId: number;\n      let dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      let width = window.innerWidth;\n      let height = window.innerHeight;\n\n      // devicePixelRatio対応のサイズ初期化\n      const updateCanvasSize = () => {\n        dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n        width = window.innerWidth;\n        height = window.innerHeight;\n        canvas.width = Math.floor(width * dpr);\n        canvas.height = Math.floor(height * dpr);\n        canvas.style.width = `${width}px`;\n        canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;\n        ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);\n      };\n\n      updateCanvasSize();\n\n      // 初期パーティクルの生成\n      const createBaseParticle = (): Particle => {\n        const currentColors = colorsRef.current;\n        const currentConfig = configRef.current;\n        const color = currentColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * currentColors.length)] || '#00f2fe';\n        return {\n          x: Math.random() * width,\n          y: Math.random() * height,\n          vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5 * currentConfig.baseSpeed,\n          vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5 * currentConfig.baseSpeed,\n          size: Math.random() * currentConfig.particleSize + 1.5,\n          baseSize: currentConfig.particleSize,\n          color,\n          alpha: Math.random() * 0.6 + 0.4,\n          life: 0,\n          maxLife: Math.random() * 300 + 200,\n          isBurst: false,\n        };\n      };\n\n      particlesRef.current = Array.from({ length: config.particleCount }, createBaseParticle);\n\n      // リサイズハンドラ\n      const handleResize = () => {\n        updateCanvasSize();\n      };\n      window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n      // ポインターイベント：canvas要素に限定しUI操作との干渉を防ぐ\n      const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        mouseRef.current.x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n        mouseRef.current.y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n        mouseRef.current.active = true;\n        mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime = performance.now();\n      };\n\n      const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const px = e.clientX - rect.left;\n        const py = e.clientY - rect.top;\n        mouseRef.current.x = px;\n        mouseRef.current.y = py;\n        mouseRef.current.active = true;\n        mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime = performance.now();\n        createBurstAt(px, py, 45);\n      };\n\n      const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n        mouseRef.current.active = false;\n      };\n\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointerleave', handlePointerLeave);\n      canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', handlePointerLeave);\n\n      // メインアニメーションループ\n      const render = () => {\n        const now = performance.now();\n        frameCountRef.current++;\n\n        // 1秒ごとにFPSとアクティブ数を更新\n        if (now - lastFpsCheckRef.current >= 1000) {\n          const fps = Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / (now - lastFpsCheckRef.current));\n          onStatsUpdate?.({\n            fps,\n            activeParticles: particlesRef.current.length,\n          });\n          frameCountRef.current = 0;\n          lastFpsCheckRef.current = now;\n        }\n\n        const curConfig = configRef.current;\n\n        // トレイル（残像）表現\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';\n        const trailAlpha = Math.max(0.04, 1.05 - curConfig.trailLength);\n        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(5, 6, 11, ${trailAlpha})`;\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n        // 加算合成（光彩エフェクト）\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n\n        // ターゲット座標の決定（ユーザー操作 or 自動周回デモ）\n        let targetX = mouseRef.current.x;\n        let targetY = mouseRef.current.y;\n        const isIdle = !mouseRef.current.active || now - mouseRef.current.lastMoveTime > 3000;\n\n        if (curConfig.autoOrbit && isIdle) {\n          autoOrbitAngleRef.current += 0.02 * curConfig.baseSpeed;\n          const angle = autoOrbitAngleRef.current;\n          // 滑らかなレムニスケート（8の字）曲線軌道\n          const scale = Math.min(width, height) * 0.32;\n          targetX = width / 2 + (scale * Math.cos(angle)) / (1 + Math.sin(angle) * Math.sin(angle));\n          targetY = height / 2 + (scale * Math.sin(angle) * Math.cos(angle)) / (1 + Math.sin(angle) * Math.sin(angle));\n        }\n\n        // パーティクル数の維持（目標数への調整）\n        const targetCount = curConfig.particleCount;\n        const currentList = particlesRef.current;\n\n        if (currentList.length < targetCount) {\n          const diff = Math.min(10, targetCount - currentList.length);\n          for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {\n     // 各パーティクルの更新と描画\n        for (let i = currentList.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n          const p = currentList[i];\n\n          if (p.isBurst) {\n            p.life++;\n            p.vx *= 0.94;\n            p.vy *= 0.94;\n            p.vy += curConfig.gravity * 0.4;\n            p.x += p.vx;\n            p.y += p.vy;\n            p.alpha = Math.max(0, 1 - p.life / p.maxLife);\n\n            if (p.life >= p.maxLife || p.alpha <= 0.01) {\n              currentList.splice(i, 1);\n              continue;\n            }\n          } else {\n            // 通常パーティクル：マウス/ターゲットへの引力スプリング\n            const dx = targetX - p.x;\n            const dy = targetY - p.y;\n            const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n            if (dist > 1) {\n              const force =\n                (curConfig.mouseFollowStrength * 0.08 * curConfig.baseSpeed) /\n                Math.max(dist * 0.04, 1);\n              p.vx += (dx / dist) * force;\n              p.vy += (dy / dist) * force;\n            }\n\n            // 速度減衰と重力\n            p.vx *= 0.96;\n            p.vy *= 0.96;\n            p.vy += curConfig.gravity * 0.2;\n\n            p.x += p.vx;\n            p.y += p.vy;\n\n            // 画面外ワープ / バウンド\n            if (p.x < 0) p.x = width;\n            if (p.x > width) p.x = 0;\n            if (p.y < 0) p.y = height;\n            if (p.y > height) p.y = 0;\n          }\n\n          // 描画\n          ctx.save();\n          ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n          ctx.shadowColor = p.color;\n          ctx.shadowBlur = p.isBurst ? 14 : 8;\n          ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n\n          const currentSize = p.isBurst\n            ? p.size\n            : curConfig.particleSize * (p.size / p.baseSize);\n\n          if (curConfig.shape === 'star') {\n            drawStar(ctx, p.x, p.y, 5, currentSize * 1.5, currentSize * 0.6);\n          } else if (curConfig.shape === 'sparkle') {\n            drawSparkle(ctx, p.x, p.y, currentSize * 1.6);\n          } else {\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, currentSize), 0, Math.PI * 2);\n            ctx.fill();\n          }\n          ctx.restore();\n        }\n\n        // 0.7;\n\n          for (let i = 0; i < maxParticlesToCheck; i++) {\n            const pA = currentList[i];\n            for (let j = i + 1; j < maxParticlesToCheck; j++) {\n              const pB = currentList[j];\n              const dx = pA.x - pB.x;\n              const dy = pA.y - pB.y;\n              const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n\n              if (dist < maxDist) {\n                const lineAlpha = (1 - dist / maxDist) * 0.35 * Math.min(pA.alpha, pB.alpha);\n                ctx.strokeStyle = pA.color;\n                ctx.globalAlpha = lineAlpha;\n                ctx.beginPath();\n                ctx.moveTo(pA.x, pA.y);\n                ctx.lineTo(pB.x, pB.y);\n                ctx.stroke();\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n
